<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01KdUxXr2D2AABBwQ2HxZBqj`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:intro}
3	
4	Consider estimating whether a content moderation policy reduces hate speech differently across demographic groups, using GPT-4 to classify thousands of posts.
5	After standard confusion-matrix correction, the analysis concludes the policy \emph{increases} hate speech among younger users---but the true effect is protective.
6	This sign reversal is not an edge case.
7	
8	Large language models (LLMs) now routinely replace human annotators in computational social science (CSS), producing labels for sentiment, hate speech, stance, and political discourse at costs orders of magnitude below manual coding~\cite{gilardi2023chatgpt,tornberg2024chatgpt,ziems2024nlp,tan2024llm}.
9	When these labels serve as effect modifiers in heterogeneous treatment effect (HTE) analysis, the standard correction protocol\footnote{Throughout, ``debiasing'' refers to correction of annotation measurement-error bias, not algorithmic fairness debiasing.} is straightforward: validate a sample against expert annotations, estimate a confusion matrix, and apply its inverse uniformly to correct conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimates.
10	Yet LLM annotations are not uniformly accurate: \citet{ashwin2025bias} document misclassification rates varying by up to 5$\times$ across demographic subgroups, consistent with $\Delta\pi > 0.20$ at typical base error rates---well above the $0.15$ threshold at which EC-HTE becomes effective; in our pilot study, GPT-4.1 achieves 85.8\% accuracy on TweetEval sentiment, but error rates range from 10.2\% to 20.0\% across text-feature subgroups ($\Delta\pi = 0.098$).
11	
12	We show that this standard correction can make things worse.
13	In 14.9\% of our synthetic configurations (across a grid of $\Delta\pi$ and $\tau(0)$ values; $\tau(0) = 0.03$), global correction reverses the sign of estimated CATE---a protective treatment ($\tau = +0.03$) appears harmful ($\hat{\tau} = -0.059$)---rising to 31\% at $\tau(0) = 0.01$ (Figure~\ref{fig:sign_reversal}); all eight real LLMs tested amplify bias under global correction, while EC-HTE eliminates sign reversal entirely.
14	Global correction amplifies bias by up to 5.9$\times$ ($K{=}4$ with CausalForest); the classical Frazis-Loeb attenuation correction~\cite{frazis2003estimating} \emph{increases} bias by up to 2.9$\times$.
15	The mechanism is over-correction: pooled correction calibrated for the average error rate introduces substantial bias in low-error subgroups where the naive estimator is nearly unbiased.
16	
17	We derive an exact bias identity explaining when and why this occurs (Theorem~\ref{thm:bias_identity}): the correction-induced bias in each subgroup factors into three independently estimable components, verified at $R^2 \geq 0.97$ on synthetic grids and 11 empirical LLM confusion matrices.
18	The identity yields a diagnostic (Proposition~\ref{prop:debiasing_hurts}) that predicts when global correction will harm a subgroup using only per-subgroup misclassification rates, without estimating any treatment effects (AUC $= 1.0$ on subgroups with non-zero effect heterogeneity; Section~\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
19	
20	The per-subgroup correction prescribed by the identity---implemented via hierarchical Bayesian confusion matrix estimation (EC-HTE)---reduces $|\text{bias}|$ from 0.059 to 0.008 under extreme heterogeneity ($K{=}2$).
21	The advantage is conditional: when $\Delta\pi$ is small, the variance cost of stratification outweighs its bias benefit.
22	
23	Existing corrections---PPI~\cite{angelopoulos2023ppi}, DSL~\cite{egami2024dsl}, PPCI~\cite{sui2026ppci}, arm-specific PPI~\cite{mozer2026ppi}, ForestIV~\cite{bu2023forestiv}---assume homogeneous prediction error; none addresses effect modifier misclassification (Section~\ref{sec:related}).
24	
25	\begin{figure*}[t]
26	\centering
27	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sign_reversal.pdf}
28	\caption{CATE sign reversal under global correction ($K{=}2$, $\tau(0) = 0.03$). (A) Naive vs.\ global-corrected CATE; red triangles mark sign reversals (7.5\% of subgroup estimates). (B) Extreme case: true $\tau = +0.03$, estimated $\hat{\tau} = -0.059$. (C) 14.9\% of configurations exhibit sign reversal; all 8 real LLMs amplify bias; EC-HTE: 0\% reversal.}
29	\label{fig:sign_reversal}
30	\end{figure*}
31	
32	Our contributions are:
33	\begin{itemize}
34	    \item An exact finite-population bias identity (Theorem~\ref{thm:bias_identity}): the bias factors into three independently estimable components ($\delta_s \cdot \Delta\tau / (1-2\bar{\pi})$), explaining sign reversal in the LLM annotation setting and enabling the diagnostic below; verified at $R^2 \geq 0.97$ (Sections~\ref{sec:theory},~\ref{sec:bias_mechanism}).
35	    \item An actionable diagnostic (Proposition~\ref{prop:debiasing_hurts}): a condition on per-subgroup misclassification rates alone predicts when global correction increases bias, without estimating any treatment effects (AUC $= 1.0$ on subgroups with non-zero effect heterogeneity)---independently useful without adopting any specific correction method (Section~\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
36	    \item One corrective instantiation of the identity: per-subgroup hierarchical Bayesian confusion matrix estimation (EC-HTE), compatible with difference-in-means, CausalForest, and R-learner, and composable with PPCI for joint covariate/outcome error---effective when error heterogeneity is sufficient (Sections~\ref{sec:method}--\ref{sec:diagnostic}).
37	\end{itemize}
38	The identity and diagnostic (contributions 1--2) are the primary results, useful without adopting any correction method; EC-HTE is one instantiation showing the identity prescribes effective correction.
39	Code and data generation scripts are publicly available.\footnote{\url{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ec-hte}}
40	
```

      "content": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:intro}\n\nConsider estimating whether a content moderation policy reduces hate speech differently across demographic groups, using GPT-4 to classify thousands of posts.\nAfter standard confusion-matrix correction, the analysis concludes the policy \\emph{increases} hate speech among younger users---but the true effect is protective.\nThis sign reversal is not an edge case.\n\nLarge language models (LLMs) now routinely replace human annotators in computational social science (CSS), producing labels for sentiment, hate speech, stance, and political discourse at costs orders of magnitude below manual coding~\\cite{gilardi2023chatgpt,tornberg2024chatgpt,ziems2024nlp,tan2024llm}.\nWhen these labels serve as effect modifiers in heterogeneous treatment effect (HTE) analysis, the standard correction protocol\\footnote{Throughout, ``debiasing'' refers to correction of annotation measurement-error bias, not algorithmic fairness debiasing.} is straightforward: validate a sample against expert annotations, estimate a confusion matrix, and apply its inverse uniformly to correct conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimates.\nYet LLM annotations are not uniformly accurate: \\citet{ashwin2025bias} document misclassification rates varying by up to 5$\\times$ across demographic subgroups, consistent with $\\Delta\\pi > 0.20$ at typical base error rates---well above the $0.15$ threshold at which EC-HTE becomes effective; in our pilot study, GPT-4.1 achieves 85.8\\% accuracy on TweetEval sentiment, but error rates range from 10.2\\% to 20.0\\% across text-feature subgroups ($\\Delta\\pi = 0.098$).\n\nWe show that this standard correction can make things worse.\nIn 14.9\\% of our synthetic configurations (across a grid of $\\Delta\\pi$ and $\\tau(0)$ values; $\\tau(0) = 0.03$), global correction reverses the sign of estimated CATE---a protective treatment ($\\tau = +0.03$) appears harmful ($\\hat{\\tau} = -0.059$)---rising to 31\\% at $\\tau(0) = 0.01$ (Figure~\\ref{fig:sign_reversal}); all eight real LLMs tested amplify bias under global correction, while EC-HTE eliminates sign reversal entirely.\nGlobal correction amplifies bias by up to 5.9$\\times$ ($K{=}4$ with CausalForest); the classical Frazis-Loeb attenuation correction~\\cite{frazis2003estimating} \\emph{increases} bias by up to 2.9$\\times$.\nThe mechanism is over-correction: pooled correction calibrated for the average error rate introduces substantial bias in low-error subgroups where the naive estimator is nearly unbiased.\n\nWe derive an exact bias identity explaining when and why this occurs (Theorem~\\ref{thm:bias_identity}): the correction-induced bias in each subgroup factors into three independently estimable components, verified at $R^2 \\geq 0.97$ on synthetic grids and 11 empirical LLM confusion matrices.\nThe identity yields a diagnostic (Proposition~\\ref{prop:debiasing_hurts}) that predicts when global correction will harm a subgroup using only per-subgroup misclassification rates, without estimating any treatment effects (AUC $= 1.0$ on subgroups with non-zero effect heterogeneity; Section~\\ref{sec:diagnostic}).\n\nThe per-subgroup correction prescribed by the identity---implemented via hierarchical Bayesian confusion matrix estimation (EC-HTE)---reduces $|\\text{bias}|$ from 0.059 to 0.008 under extreme heterogeneity ($K{=}2$).\nThe advantage is conditional: when $\\Delta\\pi$ is small, the variance cost of stratification outweighs its bias benefit.\n\nExisting corrections---PPI~\\cite{angelopoulos2023ppi}, DSL~\\cite{egami2024dsl}, PPCI~\\cite{sui2026ppci}, arm-specific PPI~\\cite{mozer2026ppi}, ForestIV~\\cite{bu2023forestiv}---assume homogeneous prediction error; none addresses effect modifier misclassification (Section~\\ref{sec:related}).\n\n\\begin{figure*}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\textwidth]{figures/fig1_sign_reversal.pdf}\n\\caption{CATE sign reversal under global correction ($K{=}2$, $\\tau(0) = 0.03$). (A) Naive vs.\\ global-corrected CATE; red triangles mark sign reversals (7.5\\% of subgroup estimates). (B) Extreme case: true $\\tau = +0.03$, estimated $\\hat{\\tau} = -0.059$. (C) 14.9\\% of configurations exhibit sign reversal; all 8 real LLMs amplify bias; EC-HTE: 0\\% reversal.}\n\\label{fig:sign_reversal}\n\\end{figure*}\n\nOur contributions are:\n\\begin{itemize}\n    \\item An exact finite-population bias identity (Theorem~\\ref{thm:bias_identity}): the bias factors into three independently estimable components ($\\delta_s \\cdot \\Delta\\tau / (1-2\\bar{\\pi})$), explaining sign reversal in the LLM annotation setting and enabling the diagnostic below; verified at $R^2 \\geq 0.97$ (Sections~\\ref{sec:theory},~\\ref{sec:bias_mechanism}).\n    \\item An actionable diagnostic (Proposition~\\ref{prop:debiasing_hurts}): a condition on per-subgroup misclassification rates alone predicts when global correction increases bias, without estimating any treatment effects (AUC $= 1.0$ on subgroups with non-zero effect heterogeneity)---independently useful without adopting any specific correction method (Section~\\ref{sec:diagnostic}).\n    \\item One corrective instantiation of the identity: per-subgroup hierarchical Bayesian confusion matrix estimation (EC-HTE), compatible with difference-in-means, CausalForest, and R-learner, and composable with PPCI for joint covariate/outcome error---effective when error heterogeneity is sufficient (Sections~\\ref{sec:method}--\\ref{sec:diagnostic}).\n\\end{itemize}\nThe identity and diagnostic (contributions 1--2) are the primary results, useful without adopting any correction method; EC-HTE is one instantiation showing the identity prescribes effective correction.\nCode and data generation scripts are publicly available.\\footnote{\\url{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ec-hte}}\n",
      "numLines": 40,

_source line 64 · 2026-05-24T14:03:19.490Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a good picture of all the figures. Let me analyze each:

**Figure 2 (method overview)** - PRIORITY: User explicitly wants PaperBanana redraw
- Major overlapping: "Hierarchical Bayes" and "Per-subgroup Corr." text overlaps
- "LLM Annotations" and "Expert Labels" boxes overlap
- "EC-HTE (Ours)" label overlaps with input boxes
- The "prior" and "vs." labels are awkwardly positioned
- Bottom summary text is disconnected
- Arrow styles inconsistent - some thick, some thin


- The comparison layout between Standard and EC-HTE is conceptually sound but poorly executed

**Figure 1 (sign reversal)** - Hero figure, needs significant refinement
- Panel B has the "Sign boundary (0)" label overlapping with the bar chart
- The danger zone shading in Panel C could be more visually refined
- Color palette works well with blue, orange, and teal
- Panel labels are clean and readable

**Figure A.1 (heatmap)** - Solid foundation
- The diverging colormap is clean and effective
- Labels clearly indicate where debiasing helps versus hurts
- Hatching effectively marks the problematic regions
- Panel title alignment and colorbar labeling need adjustment

**Figure A.2 (ROC)** - Needs work
- Relies on default matplotlib styling with standard tab10 colors
- Legend is cramped and lines lack visual distinction

**Figure A.3 (budget)** - Works reasonably well
- Four-panel layout is organized and confidence bands are visible
- Color scheme is appropriate, though crossover annotations could be clearer

**Figure A.4 (applicability frontier)** - Overcrowded
- Too many legend entries and overlapping labels like the exp-013 annotation detract from clarity

**Figure A.5 (treatment dependent misclassification)** - Generic matplotlib appearance
- Default red, blue, and green colors with repeated legends across three panels and dashed lines that don't differentiate well

**Figure A.6 (policy reversal zone)** - Solid execution
- Clean combination of line plot and heatmap with effective danger zone shading

I'm running into an issue retrieving the Google API key from the project secrets. Let me try a different approach to access it or check if there's a fallback option available. My plan is to attempt PaperBanana for Figure 2 if the key becomes available, otherwise I'll stick with matplotlib, and use SSH to render the remaining figures on the project server. I'll check for existing figure generation scripts to see how they were originally created, then fall back to matplotlib if the Google API key isn't available.
```

```text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Figure 1: CATE sign reversal under global correction — v2 optimized.
3	
4	Three-panel hero figure: (A) scatter, (B) bar chart, (C) tau sweep.
5	Designed for EMNLP full-width (figure*).
6	"""
7	import matplotlib
8	matplotlib.use('Agg')
9	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
10	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
11	import numpy as np
12	import csv
13	import os
14	
15	plt.rcParams.update({
16	    'font.family': 'serif',
17	    'font.size': 10,
18	    'axes.titlesize': 11,
19	    'axes.labelsize': 10,
20	    'xtick.labelsize': 9,
21	    'ytick.labelsize': 9,
22	    'legend.fontsize': 8.5,
23	    'mathtext.fontset': 'stix',
24	    'figure.dpi': 300,
25	    'savefig.dpi': 300,
26	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',
27	    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,
28	    'axes.spines.top': False,
29	    'axes.spines.right': False,
30	    'pdf.fonttype': 42,
31	    'ps.fonttype': 42,
32	    'lines.linewidth': 1.6,
33	})
34	
35	BASE = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0520/projects/ec_hte_llm_annotation'
36	DATA = f'{BASE}/artifacts'
37	OUT  = f'{BASE}/docs/paper/figures'
38	
39	# Semantic colors (Okabe-Ito based, muted)
40	C_NAIVE    = '#0072B2'   # blue
41	C_GLOBAL   = '#D55E00'   # vermillion
42	C_ECHTE    = '#009E73'   # green
43	C_NEUTRAL  = '#AAAAAA'   # gray for non-reversal
44	C_DANGER   = '#D55E00'   # same vermillion for danger
45	C_SAFE     = '#009E73'
46	
47	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
48	# LOAD DATA
49	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
50	rows = []
51	with open(f'{DATA}/sign_reversal_corrected.csv') as f:
52	    reader = csv.DictReader(f)
53	    for r in reader:
54	        rows.append(r)
55	
56	tau_sweep = []
57	with open(f'{DATA}/sign_reversal_tau_sweep.csv') as f:
58	    reader = csv.DictReader(f)
59	    for r in reader:
60	        tau_sweep.append(r)
61	
62	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
63	# PANEL A DATA: scatter of naive vs global-corrected
64	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
65	naive_vals = []
66	global_vals = []
67	reversals = []
68	
69	for r in rows:
70	    if r['z_value'] == '0':
71	        n = float(r['cate_naive'])
72	        g = float(r['cate_global'])
73	        rev = r['sign_reversal'] == 'True'
74	        naive_vals.append(n)
75	        global_vals.append(g)
76	        reversals.append(rev)
77	
78	naive_arr = np.array(naive_vals)
79	global_arr = np.array(global_vals)
80	rev_arr = np.array(reversals)
81	
82	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
83	# PANEL B DATA: three example cases
84	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
85	# Pick three representative sign-reversal cases with δ_π=0.25
86	panel_b_cases = []
87	target_configs = [
88	    (0.25, 0.5),   # extreme
89	    (0.25, 0.475), # moderate
90	    (0.25, 0.45),  # mild
91	]
92	
93	for dm, dc in target_configs:
94	    case = {'delta_misclass': dm, 'delta_cate': dc}
95	    for r in rows:
96	        if (abs(float(r['delta_misclass']) - dm) < 0.005 and
97	            abs(float(r['delta_cate']) - dc) < 0.005 and
98	            r['subgroup'] == 'B' and r['z_value'] == '0'):
99	            case['naive'] = float(r['cate_naive'])
100	            case['global'] = float(r['cate_global'])
101	            case['echte'] = float(r['cate_echte'])
102	            case['label'] = f'$\\delta_\\pi$={dm}\n$\\Delta\\tau$={dc:.2f}'
103	            break
104	    if 'naive' not in case:
105	        case['naive'] = 0.04
106	        case['global'] = -0.06
107	        case['echte'] = 0.03
108	        case['label'] = f'$\\delta_\\pi$={dm}\n$\\Delta\\tau$={dc}'
109	    panel_b_cases.append(case)
110	
111	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
112	# PANEL C DATA: tau sweep
113	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
114	tau_vals = []
115	rev_rates = []
116	for r in tau_sweep:
117	    tau_vals.append(float(r['tau_z0']))
118	    rev_rates.append(float(r['pct_reversal_z0']))
119	
120	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
121	# CREATE FIGURE
122	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
123	fig, (ax_a, ax_b, ax_c) = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14, 4.0),
124	    gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.15, 1.0, 1.0], 'wspace': 0.32})
125	
126	# ─── Panel A: Scatter ───
127	# Sign reversal zone background
128	ax_a.axhspan(-0.10, 0, alpha=0.06, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)
129	ax_a.axvspan(0, 0.20, alpha=0.03, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)
130	
131	# Diagonal reference
132	lims = [-0.08, 0.18]
133	ax_a.plot(lims, lims, color='#CCCCCC', lw=0.8, ls='--', zorder=1)
134	
135	# Zero lines
136	ax_a.axhline(0, color='#DDDDDD', lw=0.5, zorder=1)
137	ax_a.axvline(0, color='#DDDDDD', lw=0.5, zorder=1)
138	
139	# Data points
140	mask_same = ~rev_arr

142

60	OK_BG   = '#E8F5E9'; OK_EDGE = '#66BB6A'; OK_TXT = '#2E7D32'
61	BAD_BG  = '#FFEBEE'; BAD_EDGE= '#EF5350'; BAD_TXT= '#C62828'
62	
63	# Neutral
64	HEAD    = '#37474F'
65	ANNOT   = '#888888'
66	DIV     = '#E0E0E0'
67	
68	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
69	# LAYOUT GRID
70	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
71	COL_X = {
72	    'input': 2.0,
73	    'est':   6.0,
74	    'corr':  10.0,
75	    'inf':  14.0,
76	}
77	
78	Y_HEAD  = 7.4
79	Y_STD   = 5.6       # standard track center
80	Y_EC    = 2.4       # EC-HTE track center

82	BW_IN   = 2.2       # input box width
83	BH_S    = 0.95      # standard box height
84	BH_E    = 1.10      # EC-HTE box height (slightly taller = emphasis)
85	BH_I    = 0.80      # input box height

92	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
93	# HELPERS
94	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
95	def draw_box(cx, cy, w, h, label, sub='',
96	             fc='white', ec='gray', lw=0.8,
97	             fs=9.5, ss=7, lc='#333', sc='#777',
98	             bold=True, shadow=True, zz=3):
99	    """Draw a rounded box with optional shadow and two-line label."""
100	    x0, y0 = cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2
101	
102	    # Shadow
103	    if shadow:
104	        ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(
105	            (x0 + SHADOW_OFFSET, y0 - SHADOW_OFFSET), w, h,
106	            boxstyle=f'round,pad=0.08,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',
107	            fc=SHADOW_COLOR, ec='none', zorder=zz - 1))
108	
109	    # Main box
110	    ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(
111	        (x0, y0), w, h,
112	        boxstyle=f'round,pad=0.08,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',
113	        fc=fc, ec=ec, lw=lw, zorder=zz))
114	
115	    # Text
116	    fw = 'bold' if bold else 'normal'
117	    if sub:
118	        ax.text(cx, cy + 0.16, label, ha='center', va='center',
119	                fontsize=fs, fontweight=fw, color=lc, zorder=zz + 1)
120	        ax.text(cx, cy - 0.20, sub, ha='center', va='center',
121	                fontsize=ss, color=sc, fontstyle='italic', zorder=zz + 1)
122	    else:
123	        ax.text(cx, cy, label, ha='center', va='center',
124	                fontsize=fs, fontweight=fw, color=lc, zorder=zz + 1)
125	
126	
127	def draw_arrow(x1, y1, x2, y2, color='gray', lw=1.0, rad=0,
128	               ls='-', alpha=1.0, zz=2):
129	    """Draw a curved arrow between two points."""
130	    style = '->,head_length=4.5,head_width=2.5'
131	    conn = f'arc3,rad={rad}'
132	    ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch(
133	        (x1, y1), (x2, y2),
134	        arrowstyle=style, color=color, lw=lw,
135	        mutation_scale=9, connectionstyle=conn,
136	        linestyle=ls, alpha=alpha, zorder=zz))
137	
138	
139	def draw_badge(cx, cy, kind='check', radius=0.22, zz=6):
140	    """Draw a ✓ or ✗ badge inside a small circle at top-right corner."""
141	    # Background circle
142	    bg_color = '#E8F5E9' if kind == 'check' else '#FFEBEE'
143	    edge_color = '#43A047' if kind == 'check' else '#E53935'
144	    circle = plt.Circle((cx, cy), radius, fc=bg_color, ec=edge_color,
145	                         lw=1.0, zorder=zz, clip_on=False)
146	    ax.add_patch(circle)
147	
148	    s = radius * 0.55
149	    if kind == 'check':
150	        ax.plot([cx - s * 0.7, cx - s * 0.05, cx + s],
151	                [cy + s * 0.05, cy - s * 0.6, cy + s * 0.55],
152	                color='#43A047', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',
153	                solid_joinstyle='round', zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)
154	    else:
155	        d = s * 0.6
156	        ax.plot([cx - d, cx + d], [cy - d, cy + d],
157	                color='#E53935', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',
158	                zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)
159	        ax.plot([cx - d, cx + d], [cy + d, cy - d],
160	                color='#E53935', lw=2.0, solid_capstyle='round',
161	                zorder=zz + 1, clip_on=False)
162

164	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
165	# SWIM LANE BACKGROUNDS
166	# ══════════════════════════════════════════
167	# Standard lane
168	ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(
169	    (0.3, Y_STD - BH_S / 2 - 0.35), 15.7, BH_S + 0.70,
170	    boxstyle=f'round,pad=0,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',
171	    fc=S_BG, ec='none', zorder=0, alpha=0.7))
172	
173	# EC-HTE lane

175	    (0.3, Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.55), 15.7, BH_E + 1.10,

177	    fc=E_BG, ec='none', zorder=0, alpha=0.7))

192	        color=DIV, lw=0.6, zorder=0)

194	# Vertical column dividers (very subtle)

'#999999';  DIV = '#E0E0E0'\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# LAYOUT — wider column spacing (5 units apart)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nCOL_X = {'input': 2.5, ══════════════════════════════════════════\ndef draw_box(cx, cy, w, h, label, sub='',\n             fc='white', ec='gray', lw=0.8,\n             fs=10, ss=7.5, lc='#333', sc='#777',\n             bold=True, shadow=True, zz=3):\n    x0, y0 = cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2\n    if shadow:\n        ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch(\n            (x0 + SHADOW_OFFSET, y0 - SHADOW_OFFSET), w, h,\n fc=OK_BG, ec=OK_EDGE, lc=OK_TXT, sc='#5DA05D',\n         lw=1.2, fs=10.5, ss=8)\n\ndraw_badge(COL_X['inf'] + BW / 2 - 0.05, Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.05,\n           kind='check')\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# EC-HTE ARROWS\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.08, y_in_top,\n           COL_X['est'] - BW / 2 - 0.08, Y_EC + 0.18,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.0, rad=0.05)\n\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['input'] + BW_IN / 2 + 0.08, y_in_bot,\n           COL_X['est'] - BW / 2 - 0.08, Y_EC - 0.18,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.0, rad=-0.05)\n\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['est'] + BW / 2 + 0.08, Y_EC,\n           COL_X['corr'] - BW / 2 - 0.08, Y_EC,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\n\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['corr'] + BW / 2 + 0.08, Y_EC,\n           COL_X['inf'] - BW / 2 - 0.08, Y_EC,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=1.3)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# CROSS-TRACK \"prior\" ARROW (subtle dashed)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ny_mid = (Y_STD - BH_S / 2 + Y_EC + BH_E / 2) / 2\ndraw_arrow(COL_X['est'], Y_STD - BH_S / 2 - 0.08,\n           COL_X['est'], Y_EC + BH_E / 2 + 0.08,\n           color=E_ARR, lw=0.7, ls='--', alpha=0.35, zz=1)\n\nax.text(COL_X['est'] + 0.30, y_mid,\n        'prior', fontsize=8.5, color=E_ARR, fontstyle='italic',\n        alpha=0.55, ha='left', va='center', zorder=1)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# \"vs.\" DIVIDER\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nax.text(10.0, y_mid, 'vs.', ha='center', va='center',\n        fontsize=13, fontweight='bold', color='#D0D0D0', zorder=1)\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# ANNOTATIONS (below EC-HTE boxes, more space)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nannot_y = Y_EC - BH_E / 2 - 0.28\n\nax.text(COL_X['est'], annot_y,\n        r'Partial pooling · $\\alpha_0$ via Emp. Bayes',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=7.5, color=ANNOT,\n        fontstyle='italic')\n\nax.text(COL_X['corr'], annot_y,\n        r'$\\kappa$-guard: $\\kappa > 100$ → fallback',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=7.5, color=ANNOT,\n        fontstyle='italic')\n\nax.text(COL_X['inf'], annot_y,\n        'via CausalForestDML',\n        ha='center', va='top', fontsize=7.5, color=E_TXT,\n        fontstyle='italic')\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# RESULT HIGHLIGHT (bottom center)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nax.text(10.0, 0.10,\n        r'$\\mathbf{0\\%}$ sign reversal  ·  $|\\mathrm{bias}| \\div 7.4$',\n        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=9.5, color=OK_TXT,\n        fontweight='bold')\n\nax.text(10.0, -0.55,\n        r'Diagnostic (Prop. 2):  $|\\delta_s| \\,/\\, ((1{-}2\\bar{\\pi}) \\cdot \\pi_s) > 1$',\n        ha='center', va='center', fontsize=8, color='#E65100',\n        fontweight='bold',\n        bbox=dict(boxstyle=f'round,pad=0.25,rounding_size={ROUNDING}',\n                  fc='#FFF8E1', ec='#FFB74D', lw=0.6, alpha=0.9))\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# SAVE\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.png', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig_method_overview.pdf', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nplt.close()\nprint('Saved fig_method_overview v30')\n",

35	}

37	    'global_corrected': 'Global Corrected',
38	    'hb_ec_hte': 'HB EC-HTE',
39	    'stratified_mle': 'Stratified MLE',
40	}
41	
42	# Load data
43	df = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/ec_hte_llm_annotation/results/exp006_budget_sensitivity.csv')
44	crossover_df = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/ec_hte_llm_annotation/results/exp006_crossover.csv')
45	
46	# Filter to methods of interest
47	methods = ['global_corrected', 'hb_ec_hte', 'stratified_mle']
48	df = df[df['method'].isin(methods)].copy()
49	
50	# Compute average RMSE: mean of rmse_z0 and rmse_z1, then average across subgroups
51	df['avg_rmse'] = (df['rmse_z0'] + df['rmse_z1']) / 2.0
52	
53	agg = df.groupby(['regime', 'n_expert', 'k_subgroups', 'method']).agg(
54	    mean_rmse=('avg_rmse', 'mean'),
55	    std_rmse=('avg_rmse', 'std'),
56	).reset_index()
57	
58	# Panel layout: rows=K(2,4), cols=regime(extreme, moderate)
59	fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(7, 3), sharex=True)
60	
61	panel_configs = [
62	    (0, 0, 2, 'extreme'),
63	    (0, 1, 2, 'moderate'),
64	    (1, 0, 4, 'extreme'),
65	    (1, 1, 4, 'moderate'),
66	]
67	
68	panel_titles = {
69	    (2, 'extreme'): 'K=2, Extreme',
70	    (2, 'moderate'): 'K=2, Moderate',
71	    (4, 'extreme'): 'K=4, Extreme',
72	    (4, 'moderate'): 'K=4, Moderate',
73	}
74	
75	ylim_caps = {2: 0.45, 4: 0.55}
76	
77	for row, col, k, regime in panel_configs:
78	    ax = axes[row, col]

80	
81	    for method in methods:
82	        mdata = subset[subset['method'] == method].sort_values('n_expert')
83	        rmse_vals = mdata['mean_rmse'].values
84	        std_vals = mdata['std_rmse'].values
85	        n_vals = mdata['n_expert'].values
86

88	            n_vals, rmse_vals,

90	            label=LABELS[method], marker='o', markersize=4,
91	        )
92	        ax.fill_between(

94	            np.clip(rmse_vals - std_vals, 0, None),

98

100	    ax.set_xticks([50, 100, 250, 500, 1000, 2000])
101	    ax.get_xaxis().set_major_formatter(matplotlib.ticker.ScalarFormatter())
102	    ax.set_ylim(0, ylim_caps[k])
103	    ax.set_title(panel_titles[(k, regime)], fontsize=10, pad=4)
104

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""
3	exp005_diagnostic_criterion.py - Diagnostic criterion for when global correction hurts
4	
5	Evaluates Claim 3: global correction introduces harmful bias at subgroups where
6	π_s < π̄, proportional to (π̄ - π_s) × Δτ.
7	
8	Two levels of analysis:
9	  A) Setting-level: per (regime, K, n_expert), is global worse than naive on average?
10	     Predictor: max_s[(π̄ − π_s) × Δτ] across subgroups (worst-case signed product).
11	  B) Subgroup-level: per (setting, subgroup), does global introduce more bias than naive?
12	     Predictor: (π̄ − π_s) × Δτ (signed: positive when subgroup has low misclass).
13	
14	Input:  results/exp001_hb_results_v2.csv, results/exp003_phase_a_v2.csv,
15	        optionally results/exp007_ppci_dsl.csv (--include-exp007)
16	Output: results/exp005_diagnostic.csv, results/exp005_diagnostic_subgroups.csv,
17	        results/exp005_roc.png
18	"""
19	
20	import argparse
21	import os
22	import warnings
23	import numpy as np
24	import pandas as pd
25	from sklearn.metrics import roc_curve, auc
26	
27	warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
28	
29	RESULTS_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'results')
30	
31	# ── DGP parameters (mirrored from exp001/exp003/exp007) ────────────────────
32	
33	EXP001_REGIMES = {
34	    2: {
35	        'extreme':     {'misclass': [0.25, 0.05], 'tau_z1': 0.5, 'tau_z0': 0.1},
36	        'moderate':    {'misclass': [0.15, 0.10], 'tau_z1': 0.3, 'tau_z0': 0.15},
37	        'homogeneous': {'misclass': [0.12, 0.12], 'tau_z1': 0.25, 'tau_z0': 0.20},
38	    },
39	    4: {
40	        'extreme':     {'misclass': [0.30, 0.20, 0.10, 0.03], 'tau_z1': 0.5, 'tau_z0': 0.1},
41	        'moderate':    {'misclass': [0.20, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05], 'tau_z1': 0.3, 'tau_z0': 0.15},
42	        'homogeneous': {'misclass': [0.12, 0.12, 0.12, 0.12], 'tau_z1': 0.25, 'tau_z0': 0.20},
43	    },
44	}
45

1	"""
2	Applicability Frontier: When does EC-HTE help?
3	Combines data from exp-001, exp-003, exp-013, exp-015.
4	"""
5	
6	import pandas as pd
7	import numpy as np
8	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
9	import matplotlib.patches as mpatches
10	from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
11	import warnings
12	warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
13	
14	plt.rcParams.update({
15	    'font.family': 'serif',
16	    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'DejaVu Serif'],
17	    'mathtext.fontset': 'dejavuserif',
18	    'font.size': 10,
19	    'axes.labelsize': 12,
20	    'axes.titlesize': 13,
21	    'legend.fontsize': 8.5,
22	    'xtick.labelsize': 9,
23	    'ytick.labelsize': 9,
24	    'figure.dpi': 150,
25	})

27	# ── Data extraction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
28	
29	def load_exp001():
30	    df = pd.read_csv('results/exp001_hb_results_v2.csv')
31	    df = df[df['regime'] == 'extreme']
32	    df['avg_rmse'] = (df['rmse_z0'] + df['rmse_z1']) / 2
33	
34	    rows = []
35	    for k in [2, 4]:
36	        worst_misclass = {2: 0.25, 4: 0.30}[k]
37	        hb = df[(df['method'] == 'hb_dirichlet') & (df['k_subgroups'] == k)]
38	        gl = df[(df['method'] == 'global_corrected') & (df['k_subgroups'] == k)]
39	        hb_avg = hb.groupby('n_expert')['avg_rmse'].mean()
40	        gl_avg = gl.groupby('n_expert')['avg_rmse'].mean()
41	        for n in hb_avg.index:
42	            ratio = hb_avg[n] / gl_avg[n]
43	            rows.append({
44	                'exp': f'exp-001 (K={k})',
45	                'n_per_K': n / k,
46	                'worst_misclass': worst_misclass,
47	                'hb_rmse': hb_avg[n],
48	                'gl_rmse': gl_avg[n],
49	                'ratio': ratio,
50	            })

      "content": "\"\"\"\nApplicability Frontier: When does EC-HTE help?\nCombines data from exp-001, exp-003, exp-013, exp-015.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nfrom matplotlib.lines import Line2D\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'DejaVu Serif'],\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'dejavuserif',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'legend.fontsize': 8.5,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 9,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 150,\n})\n\n# ── Data extraction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_exp001():\n    df = pd.read_csv('results/exp001_hb_results_v2.csv')\n    df = df[df['regime'] == 'extreme']\n    df['avg_rmse'] = (df['rmse_z0'] + df['rmse_z1']) / 2\n\n    rows = []\n    for k in [2, 4]:\n        worst_misclass = {2: 0.25, 4: 0.30}[k]\n        hb = df[(df['method'] == 'hb_dirichlet') & (df['k_subgroups'] == k)]\n        gl = df[(df['method'] == 'global_corrected') & (df['k_subgroups'] == k)]\n        hb_avg = hb.groupby('n_expert')['avg_rmse'].mean()\n        gl_avg = gl.groupby('n_expert')['avg_rmse'].mean()\n        for n in hb_avg.index:\n            ratio = hb_avg[n] / gl_avg[n]\n            rows.append({\n                'exp': f'exp-001 (K={k})',\n                'n_per_K': n / k,\n                'worst_misclass': worst_misclass,\n                'hb_rmse': hb_avg[n],\n                'gl_rmse': gl_avg[n],\n                'ratio': ratio,\n            })",
      "numLines": 50,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 267
    }
  },

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""exp_treatment_dep_misclass.py — Treatment-dependent misclassification sensitivity
3	
4	Tests EC-HTE robustness when P(Ẑ|Z,T) ≠ P(Ẑ|Z).
5	Violation: P(Ẑ≠Z | Z,S,T) = π_s + γ_T·(T - 0.5)
6	
7	DGP: K=2, pi=[0.05,0.25], tau(z=0)=0.1, tau(z=1)=0.5, N=5000, n_expert=250
8	γ_T ∈ {0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20}
9	100 MC runs per γ_T
10	
11	Methods: naive, global_corrected, hb_ec_hte (centered EB)
12	"""
13	
14	import os
15	import time
16	import warnings
17	import numpy as np
18	import pandas as pd
19	from scipy.special import gammaln
20	from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar
21	
22	warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
23	
24	N = 5000
25	K = 2
26	N_EXPERT = 250
27	PI = [0.05, 0.25]
28	TAU_TRUE = {0: 0.1, 1: 0.5}
29	GAMMAS = [0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20]
30	N_MC = 100
31	LABELS = ['A', 'B']
32	
33	ARTIFACTS_DIR = os.path.expanduser(
34	    '~/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0520/projects/ec_hte_llm_annotation/artifacts')
35	
36	
37	def generate_data(seed, gamma_T):
38	    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
39	    S = rng.choice(K, N, p=[0.5, 0.5])
40	    T = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, N)
41	    Z_true = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, N)
42	
43	    tau = np.array([TAU_TRUE[z] for z in Z_true])
44	    baseline = 1.0 + 0.5 * Z_true
45	    Y = baseline + tau * T + rng.normal(0, 1, N)
46	
47	    Z_hat = Z_true.copy()
48	    for s in range(K):
49	        mask_s = S == s
50	        pi_eff = PI[s] + gamma_T * (T[mask_s] - 0.5)
```

1	"""
2	Task J: Policy Reversal Zone τ Sweep
3	
4	Sweeps τ(0) values and plots "policy reversal probability vs true CATE magnitude".
5	Sign reversal = CATE estimate has opposite sign to true CATE.
6	"""
7	
8	import numpy as np
9	import pandas as pd
10	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
11	import matplotlib.ticker as mticker
12	from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap
13	import os
14	
15	# ── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
16	BASE_MISCLASS = 0.15
17	TAU_Z0_SWEEP = [0.01, 0.02, 0.03, 0.05, 0.07, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20]
18	ARTIFACTS_DIR = os.path.expanduser(
19	    '~/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0520/projects/ec_hte_llm_annotation/artifacts')
20	os.makedirs(ARTIFACTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)
21	
22	# ── Load exp002 CSV ─────────────────────────────────────────────
23	df_all = pd.read_csv('results/exp002_bias_bound.csv')
24	df_z0 = df_all[df_all['z_value'] == 0].copy()
25	
26	# Compute naive bias: bias_naive(z=0, s) = π_s × Δτ
27	# π_A = BASE + dm/2, π_B = BASE - dm/2
28	df_z0['pi_s'] = np.where(
29	    df_z0['subgroup'] == 'A',
30	    BASE_MISCLASS + df_z0['delta_misclass'] / 2.0,
31	    BASE_MISCLASS - df_z0['delta_misclass'] / 2.0
32	)
33	df_z0['bias_naive'] = df_z0['pi_s'] * df_z0['delta_cate']
34	df_z0['bias_global'] = df_z0['analytic_bias']
35	
36	# ── Sweep τ(0) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
37	rows = []
38	for tau0 in TAU_Z0_SWEEP:
39	    for _, r in df_z0.iterrows():
40	        dm = r['delta_misclass']
41	        dc = r['delta_cate']
42	        sg = r['subgroup']
43	
44	        true_cate = tau0  # τ(0) > 0 always in our sweep
45	
46	        est_naive = tau0 + r['bias_naive']
47	        est_global = tau0 + r['bias_global']
48	
49	        rev_naive = (true_cate > 0 and est_naive < 0) or (true_cate < 0 and est_naive > 0)
50	        rev_global = (true_cate > 0 and est_global < 0) or (true_cate < 0 and est_global > 0)

abs(float(r['delta_cate']) - dc) < 0.005 and\n            r['subgroup'] == 'B' and r['z_value'] == '0'):\n            case['naive'] = float(r['cate_naive'])\n            case['global'] = 'naive' not in case:\n        case['naive'] = 0.04\n        case['global'] = -0.06\n        case['echte'] = 0.03\n        case['label'] = f'$\\\\delta_\\\\pi$={dm}\\n$\\\\Delta\\\\tau$={dc}\\nsub B'\n    panel_b_cases.append(case)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL C DATA\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ntau_vals, rev_rates = [], []\nfor r in tau_sweep:\n estimate')\nax_a.set_xlim(-0.07, 0.18)\nax_a.set_ylim(-0.08, 0.17)\nax_a.legend(loc='upper left', framealpha=0.92, edgecolor='none',\n            markerscale=1.8, handletextpad=0.4)\nax_a.text(-0.02, ══════════════════════════════════════════\nrows = []\nwith open(f'{DATA}/sign_reversal_corrected.csv') as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for r in reader:\n        rows.append(r)\n\ntau_sweep = []\nwith open(f'{DATA}/sign_reversal_tau_sweep.csv') as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for r in reader:\n        tau_sweep.append(r)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL A DATA\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nnaive_vals, global_vals, reversals = [], [], []\nfor r in rows:\n    if r['z_value'] == '0':\n        naive_vals.append(float(r['cate_naive']))\n        global_vals.append(float(r['cate_global']))\n        reversals.append(r['sign_reversal'] == 'True')\n\nnaive_arr = np.array(naive_vals)\nglobal_arr = np.array(global_vals)\nrev_arr = np.array(reversals)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL B DATA\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\npanel_b_cases = []\ntarget_configs = [(0.25, 0.5), (0.25, 0.475), (0.25, 0.45)]\n\nfor dm, dc in target_configs:\n    case = {'delta_misclass': dm, 'delta_cate': dc}\n    for r in rows:\n        if (abs(float(r['delta_misclass']) - dm) < 0.005 and\n            abs(float(r['delta_cate']) - dc) < 0.005 and\n            r['subgroup'] == 'B' and r['z_value'] == '0'):\n            case['naive'] = float(r['cate_naive'])\n            case['global'] = float(r['cate_global'])\n            case['echte'] = float(r['cate_echte'])\n            case['label'] = f'$\\\\delta_\\\\pi$={dm}\\n$\\\\Delta\\\\tau$={dc:.2f}\\nsub B'\n            break\n    if 'naive' not in case:\n        case['naive'] = 0.04\n        case['global'] = -0.06\n        case['echte'] = 0.03\n        case['label'] = f'$\\\\delta_\\\\pi$={dm}\\n$\\\\Delta\\\\tau$={dc}\\nsub B'\n    panel_b_cases.append(case)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL C DATA\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ntau_vals, rev_rates = [], []\nfor r in tau_sweep:\n    tau_vals.append(float(r['tau_z0']))\n    rev_rates.append(float(r['pct_reversal_z0']))\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# CREATE FIGURE\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfig, (ax_a, ax_b, ax_c) = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14, 3.8),\n    gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.15, 1.0, 1.0], 'wspace': 0.34})\n\n# ─── Panel A: Scatter ───\nax_a.axhspan(-0.10, 0, alpha=0.05, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)\nax_a.axvspan(0, 0.20, alpha=0.025, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)\n\nlims = [-0.08, 0.18]\nax_a.plot(lims, lims, color='#D0D0D0', lw=0.8, ls='--', zorder=1)\nax_a.axhline(0, color='#E0E0E0', lw=0.4, zorder=1)\nax_a.axvline(0, color='#E0E0E0', lw=0.4, zorder=1)\n\nmask_same = ~rev_arr\nmask_rev = rev_arr\n\nax_a.scatter(naive_arr[mask_same], global_arr[mask_same],\n             s=6, c=C_NEUTRAL, alpha=0.20, edgecolors='none',\n             zorder=2, label='Same sign', rasterized=True)\nax_a.scatter(naive_arr[mask_rev], global_arr[mask_rev],\n             s=12, c=C_DANGER, alpha=0.40, edgecolors='none',\n             marker='^', zorder=3, label='Sign reversal', rasterized=True)\n\nt = ax_a.text(0.12, -0.055, 'Quadrant IV\\n(sign reversal)',\n              fontsize=7.5, color=C_DANGER, alpha=0.6, ha='center',\n              fontstyle='italic')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2, foreground='white')])\n\nax_a.set_xlabel('Naive CATE estimate')\nax_a.set_ylabel('Global-corrected CATE estimate')\nax_a.set_xlim(-0.07, 0.18)\nax_a.set_ylim(-0.08, 0.17)\nax_a.legend(loc='upper left', framealpha=0.92, edgecolor='none',\n            markerscale=1.8, handletextpad=0.4)\nax_a.text(-0.02, 1.04, 'A', transform=ax_a.transAxes,\n          fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', va='bottom')\n\n# ─── Panel B: Bar chart ───\ntrue_cate = 0.03\nx_pos = np.arange(len(panel_b_cases))\nbar_w = 0.22\n\nax_b.axhline(true_cate, color='#888888', ls='--', lw=0.7, zorder=1)\nax_b.axhline(0, color=C_DANGER, ls=':', lw=0.6, alpha=0.4, zorder=1)\n\nt1 = ax_b.text(len(panel_b_cases) - 0.3, true_cate + 0.0025,\n               f'True CATE ({true_cate})', fontsize=7, color='#666666',\n               ha='right', va='bottom')\nt1.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2, foreground='white')])\n\nt2 = ax_b.text(len(panel_b_cases) - 0.3, 0.0025,\n               'Sign boundary (0)', fontsize=6.5, color=C_DANGER,\n               ha='right', va='bottom', alpha=0.5)\nt2.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2, foreground='white')])\n\nax_b.bar(x_pos - bar_w, [c['naive'] for c in panel_b_cases],\n         bar_w, color=C_NAIVE, alpha=0.85, label='Naive',\n         edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, zorder=2)\nax_b.bar(x_pos, [c['global'] for c in panel_b_cases],\n         bar_w, color=C_GLOBAL, alpha=0.85, label='Global-corrected',\n         edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, zorder=2)\nax_b.bar(x_pos + bar_w, [c['echte'] for c in panel_b_cases],\n         bar_w, color=C_ECHTE, alpha=0.85, label='EC-HTE',\n         edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, zorder=2)\n\nax_b.set_xticks(x_pos)\nax_b.set_xticklabels([c.get('label', '') for c in panel_b_cases],\n                      fontsize=7, linespacing=1.2)\nax_b.set_ylabel('CATE estimate')\nax_b.legend(loc='upper left', framealpha=0.92, edgecolor='none',\n            fontsize=7.5, handlelength=1.2)\nax_b.set_xlim(-0.55, len(panel_b_cases) - 0.45)\nax_b.text(-0.02, 1.04, 'B', transform=ax_b.transAxes,\n          fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', va='bottom')\n\n# ─── Panel C: Sign reversal rate sweep ───\ndanger_mask = np.array(rev_rates) > 0\nif any(danger_mask):\n    max_tau_danger = max(t for t, r in zip(tau_vals, rev_rates) if r > 0)\n    ax_c.axvspan(0, max_tau_danger + 0.01, alpha=0.05, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)\n    t3 = ax_c.text(0.025, max(rev_rates) * 0.62, 'Danger\\nzone',\n                   fontsize=8, color=C_DANGER, alpha=0.35, ha='left',\n                   va='center', fontstyle='italic')\n\nax_c.plot(tau_vals, rev_rates, 'o-', color=C_DANGER, markersize=5.5,\n          markerfacecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.4,\n          markeredgecolor=C_DANGER, zorder=3, label='Sign reversal rate')\n\nax_c.axhline(0, color='#E0E0E0', lw=0.4)\n\nax_c.text(0.97, 0.95,\n          f'Max |bias| = 0.089\\n$\\\\tau(0) = 0.03$',\n          transform=ax_c.transAxes, fontsize=7, color='#777777',\n          ha='right', va='top',\n          bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', fc='white', ec='#E0E0E0',\n                    lw=0.4, alpha=0.92))\n\nax_c.set_xlabel(r'True CATE $\\tau(0)$')\nax_c.set_ylabel('Sign reversal rate     "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Figure 1: CATE sign reversal under global correction — v2 optimized.\n\nThree-panel hero figure: (A) scatter, (B) bar chart, (C) tau sweep.\nDesigned for EMNLP full-width (figure*).\n\"\"\"\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\nimport csv\nimport os\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'axes.titlesize': 11,\n    'axes.labelsize': 10,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 9,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 9,\n    'legend.fontsize': 8.5,\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'stix',\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42,\n    'ps.fonttype': 42,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.6,\n})\n\nBASE = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0520/projects/ec_hte_llm_annotation'\nDATA = f'{BASE}/artifacts'\nOUT  = f'{BASE}/docs/paper/figures'\n\n# Semantic colors (Okabe-Ito based, muted)\nC_NAIVE    = '#0072B2'   # blue\nC_GLOBAL   = '#D55E00'   # vermillion\nC_ECHTE    = '#009E73'   # green\nC_NEUTRAL  = '#AAAAAA'   # gray for non-reversal\nC_DANGER   = '#D55E00'   # same vermillion for danger\nC_SAFE     = '#009E73'\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# LOAD DATA\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nrows = []\nwith open(f'{DATA}/sign_reversal_corrected.csv') as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for r in reader:\n        rows.append(r)\n\ntau_sweep = []\nwith open(f'{DATA}/sign_reversal_tau_sweep.csv') as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for r in reader:\n        tau_sweep.append(r)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL A DATA: scatter of naive vs global-corrected\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nnaive_vals = []\nglobal_vals = []\nreversals = []\n\nfor r in rows:\n    if r['z_value'] == '0':\n        n = float(r['cate_naive'])\n        g = float(r['cate_global'])\n        rev = r['sign_reversal'] == 'True'\n        naive_vals.append(n)\n        global_vals.append(g)\n        reversals.append(rev)\n\nnaive_arr = np.array(naive_vals)\nglobal_arr = np.array(global_vals)\nrev_arr = np.array(reversals)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL B DATA: three example cases\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# Pick three representative sign-reversal cases with δ_π=0.25\npanel_b_cases = []\ntarget_configs = [\n    (0.25, 0.5),   # extreme\n    (0.25, 0.475), # moderate\n    (0.25, 0.45),  # mild\n]\n\nfor dm, dc in target_configs:\n    case = {'delta_misclass': dm, 'delta_cate': dc}\n    for r in rows:\n        if (abs(float(r['delta_misclass']) - dm) < 0.005 and\n            abs(float(r['delta_cate']) - dc) < 0.005 and\n            r['subgroup'] == 'B' and r['z_value'] == '0'):\n            case['naive'] = float(r['cate_naive'])\n            case['global'] = float(r['cate_global'])\n            case['echte'] = float(r['cate_echte'])\n            case['label'] = f'$\\\\delta_\\\\pi$={dm}\\n$\\\\Delta\\\\tau$={dc:.2f}'\n            break\n    if 'naive' not in case:\n        case['naive'] = 0.04\n        case['global'] = -0.06\n        case['echte'] = 0.03\n        case['label'] = f'$\\\\delta_\\\\pi$={dm}\\n$\\\\Delta\\\\tau$={dc}'\n    panel_b_cases.append(case)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# PANEL C DATA: tau sweep\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\ntau_vals = []\nrev_rates = []\nfor r in tau_sweep:\n    tau_vals.append(float(r['tau_z0']))\n    rev_rates.append(float(r['pct_reversal_z0']))\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# CREATE FIGURE\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfig, (ax_a, ax_b, ax_c) = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14, 4.0),\n    gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.15, 1.0, 1.0], 'wspace': 0.32})\n\n# ─── Panel A: Scatter ───\n# Sign reversal zone background\nax_a.axhspan(-0.10, 0, alpha=0.06, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)\nax_a.axvspan(0, 0.20, alpha=0.03, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)\n\n# Diagonal reference\nlims = [-0.08, 0.18]\nax_a.plot(lims, lims, color='#CCCCCC', lw=0.8, ls='--', zorder=1)\n\n# Zero lines\nax_a.axhline(0, color='#DDDDDD', lw=0.5, zorder=1)\nax_a.axvline(0, color='#DDDDDD', lw=0.5, zorder=1)\n\n# Data points\nmask_same = ~rev_arr\nmask_rev = rev_arr\n\nax_a.scatter(naive_arr[mask_same], global_arr[mask_same],\n             s=8, c=C_NEUTRAL, alpha=0.25, edgecolors='none',\n             zorder=2, label='Same sign', rasterized=True)\nax_a.scatter(naive_arr[mask_rev], global_arr[mask_rev],\n             s=10, c=C_DANGER, alpha=0.35, edgecolors='none',\n             marker='^', zorder=3, label='Sign reversal', rasterized=True)\n\n# Zone labels\nax_a.text(0.12, -0.055, 'Quadrant IV\\n(sign reversal)',\n          fontsize=7.5, color=C_DANGER, alpha=0.7, ha='center',\n          fontstyle='italic')\n\nax_a.set_xlabel('Naive CATE estimate')\nax_a.set_ylabel('Global-corrected CATE estimate')\nax_a.set_xlim(-0.07, 0.18)\nax_a.set_ylim(-0.08, 0.17)\nax_a.legend(loc='upper left', framealpha=0.9, edgecolor='none',\n            markerscale=1.5)\nax_a.set_title('A', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=13)\n\n# ─── Panel B: Bar chart ───\ntrue_cate = 0.03\nx_pos = np.arange(len(panel_b_cases))\nbar_w = 0.22\n\n# Reference lines\nax_b.axhline(true_cate, color='#666666', ls='--', lw=0.8, zorder=1)\nax_b.axhline(0, color=C_DANGER, ls=':', lw=0.7, alpha=0.5, zorder=1)\n\n# True CATE label\nax_b.text(len(panel_b_cases) - 0.15, true_cate + 0.002,\n          f'True CATE ({true_cate})', fontsize=7.5, color='#666666',\n          ha='right', va='bottom')\n\n# Sign boundary label\nax_b.text(len(panel_b_cases) - 0.15, 0.002,\n          'Sign boundary (0)', fontsize=7, color=C_DANGER,\n          ha='right', va='bottom', alpha=0.6)\n\nbars_n = ax_b.bar(x_pos - bar_w,\n                   [c['naive'] for c in panel_b_cases],\n                   bar_w, color=C_NAIVE, alpha=0.85, label='Naive',\n                   edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, zorder=2)\nbars_g = ax_b.bar(x_pos,\n                   [c['global'] for c in panel_b_cases],\n                   bar_w, color=C_GLOBAL, alpha=0.85, label='Global-corrected',\n                   edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, zorder=2)\nbars_e = ax_b.bar(x_pos + bar_w,\n                   [c['echte'] for c in panel_b_cases],\n                   bar_w, color=C_ECHTE, alpha=0.85, label='EC-HTE',\n                   edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5, zorder=2)\n\nax_b.set_xticks(x_pos)\nax_b.set_ylabel('CATE estimate')\nax_b.legend(loc='upper left', framealpha=0.9, edgecolor='none',\n            fontsize=8)\nax_b.set_title('B', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=13)\nax_b.set_xlim(-0.5, len(panel_b_cases) - 0.5)\n\nax_b.set_xticklabels([c.get('label', '') + '\\nsub B' for c in panel_b_cases],\n                      fontsize=7.5, linespacing=1.3)\n\n# ─── Panel C: Sign reversal rate sweep ───\n# Danger zone background\ndanger_mask = np.array(rev_rates) > 0\nif any(danger_mask):\n    max_tau_danger = max(t for t, r in zip(tau_vals, rev_rates) if r > 0)\n    ax_c.axvspan(0, max_tau_danger + 0.01, alpha=0.06, color=C_DANGER, zorder=0)\n    ax_c.text(0.02, max(rev_rates) * 0.65, 'Danger\\nzone',\n              fontsize=8.5, color=C_DANGER, alpha=0.45, ha='left',\n              va='center', fontstyle='italic')\n\nax_c.plot(tau_vals, rev_rates, 'o-', color=C_DANGER, markersize=6,\n          markerfacecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.5,\n          markeredgecolor=C_DANGER, zorder=3, label='Sign reversal rate')\n\n# Annotate max bias\nmax_bias_text = 'Max |bias| = 0.089'\nax_c.axhline(0, color='#DDDDDD', lw=0.5)\n\nax_c.text(0.97, 0.95, f'{max_bias_text}\\n$\\\\tau(0) = 0.03$',\n          transform=ax_c.transAxes, fontsize=7.5, color='#666666',\n          ha='right', va='top',\n          bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', fc='white', ec='#DDDDDD',\n                    lw=0.5, alpha=0.9))\n\nax_c.set_xlabel(r'True CATE $\\tau(0)$')\nax_c.set_ylabel('Sign reversal rate (%)')\nax_c.legend(loc='upper right', framealpha=0.9, edgecolor='none',\n            bbox_to_anchor=(0.98, 0.75))\nax_c.set_title('C', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=13)\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\n# SAVE\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig1_sign_reversal.png', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nfig.savefig(f'{OUT}/fig1_sign_reversal.pdf', dpi=300, facecolor='white')\nplt.close()\nprint('Saved fig1_sign_reversal v2')\n",

  },

202	# ── Data loading: subgroup-level ───────────────────────────────────────────
203	
204	def _build_subgroup_row(source, regime, k, n_exp, s_label, pi_s_val,
205	                        pi_bar, delta_tau, naive_bias_z0, glob_bias_z0):
206	    """Build one subgroup-level row."""
207	    signed_product = (pi_bar - pi_s_val) * delta_tau
208	    theory_bias = abs((pi_s_val - pi_bar) * delta_tau / (1.0 - 2.0 * pi_bar))
209	    hurts = abs(glob_bias_z0) > abs(naive_bias_z0)
210	
211	    return {
212	        'setting_id': f"{source}_K{k}_{regime}_n{n_exp}_{s_label}" if source != 'exp003'
213	                      else f"exp003_{regime}_n{n_exp}_{s_label}",
214	        'source': source, 'regime': regime,
215	        'k_subgroups': k, 'n_expert': n_exp,
216	        'subgroup': str(s_label),
217	        'pi_s': pi_s_val, 'pi_bar': pi_bar,
218	        'delta_s_signed': pi_bar - pi_s_val,
219	        'delta_tau': delta_tau,
220	        'signed_product': signed_product,
221	        'unsigned_product': abs(pi_bar - pi_s_val) * delta_tau,
222	        'theory_bias_magnitude': theory_bias,
223	        'naive_abs_bias_z0': abs(naive_bias_z0),
224	        'global_abs_bias_z0': abs(glob_bias_z0),
225	        'hurts_at_subgroup': hurts,
226	    }
227	
228	
229	def load_exp001_subgroups():
230	    """Load exp001 subgroup-level data."""
231	    for fname in ['exp001_hb_results_v2.csv', 'exp001_hb_results.csv']:
232	        path = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, fname)
233	        if os.path.exists(path):
234	            df = pd.read_csv(path)
235	            break
236	    else:
237	        return pd.DataFrame()
238	
239	    labels_map = {2: ['A', 'B'], 4: ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D']}
240	    rows = []
241	    for (regime, k, n_exp), grp in df.groupby(['regime', 'k_subgroups', 'n_expert']):
242	        params = EXP001_REGIMES[k][regime]
243	        pi_s = np.array(params['misclass'])
244	        pi_bar = np.mean(pi_s)
245	        delta_tau = abs(params['tau_z1'] - params['tau_z0'])
246	        labels = labels_map[k]
247	
248	        for idx, s_label in enumerate(labels):
249	            naive_s = grp[(grp['method'] == 'naive') & (grp['subgroup'] == s_label)]
250	            glob_s = grp[(grp['method'] == 'global_corrected') & (grp['subgroup'] == s_label)]
251	            if naive_s.empty or glob_s.empty:
252	                continue
253	            rows.append(_build_subgroup_row(
254	                'exp001', regime, k, n_exp, s_label,
255	                pi_s[idx], pi_bar, delta_tau,
256	                naive_s['bias_z0'].values[0], glob_s['bias_z0'].values[0]))
257	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
258	
259	
260	def load_exp003_subgroups():
261	    """Load exp003 subgroup-level data."""
262	    path = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, 'exp003_phase_a_v2.csv')
263	    if not os.path.exists(path):
264	        return pd.DataFrame()
265	
266	    df = pd.read_csv(path)

273	        pi_s_arr = np.array([pi_s_dict[s] for s in subgroups])
274	        pi_bar = np.mean(pi_s_arr)
275	
276	        for idx, s_label in enumerate(subgroups):
277	            delta_tau_s = EXP003_DELTA_S[s_label]
278	            naive_s = grp[(grp['method'] == 'naive') & (grp['level'] == s_label)]
279	            glob_s = grp[(grp['method'] == 'global_corrected') & (grp['level'] == s_label)]
280	            if naive_s.empty or glob_s.empty:
281	                continue
282	            rows.append(_build_subgroup_row(
283	                'exp003', regime, 4, n_exp, s_label,
284	                pi_s_arr[idx], pi_bar, delta_tau_s,
285	                naive_s['bias_z0'].values[0], glob_s['bias_z0'].values[0]))
286	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
287	
288	
289	def load_exp007_subgroups():
290	    """Load exp007 subgroup-level data (DGP A only)."""
291	    path = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, 'exp007_ppci_dsl.csv')
292	    if not os.path.exists(path):
293	        return pd.DataFrame()
294	
295	    df = pd.read_csv(path)
296	    df = df[df['dgp'] == 'A_covariate_only']
297	
298	    rows = []
299	    for (regime, k, n_exp), grp in df.groupby(['regime', 'k_subgroups', 'n_expert']):
300	        params = EXP007_REGIMES[regime]
301	        pi_s = np.array(EXP007_MISCLASS_K2[regime])
302	        pi_bar = np.mean(pi_s)
303	        delta_tau = abs(params['tau_z1'] - params['tau_z0'])
304	        sub_labels = sorted(grp['subgroup'].unique())
305	
306	        for idx, s_label in enumerate(sub_labels):
307	            if idx >= len(pi_s):
308	                continue
309	            naive_s = grp[(grp['method'] == 'naive') & (grp['subgroup'] == s_label)]
310	            glob_s = grp[(grp['method'] == 'global_corrected') & (grp['subgroup'] == s_label)]
311	            if naive_s.empty or glob_s.empty:
312	                continue
313	            rows.append(_build_subgroup_row(
314	                'exp007', regime, k, n_exp, s_label,
315	                pi_s[idx], pi_bar, delta_tau,
316	                naive_s['bias_z0'].values[0], glob_s['bias_z0'].values[0]))
317	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
318	
319	
320	# ── Diagnostic evaluation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
321	
322	def find_optimal_threshold(scores, labels):
323	    """Find threshold that maximizes Youden's J statistic."""
324	    if len(np.unique(labels)) < 2:
325	        return np.median(scores), 0.5
326	    fpr, tpr, thresholds = roc_curve(labels, scores)
327	    best_idx = np.argmax(tpr - fpr)
328	    return thresholds[best_idx], auc(fpr, tpr)
329	
330	
331	def loso_cv(scores, labels):
332	    """Leave-one-out CV. Returns AUC and per-fold accuracy."""
333	    n = len(scores)
334	    if len(np.unique(labels)) < 2:
335	        return 0.5, 0.0, (np.array([0, 1]), np.array([0, 1]), np.array([0]))
336	
337	    fpr, tpr, thresholds = roc_curve(labels, scores)
338	    cv_auc = auc(fpr, tpr)
339	
340	    correct = 0
341	    for i in range(n):
342	        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
343	        mask[i] = False
344	        if len(np.unique(labels[mask])) < 2:
345	            continue
346	        t, _ = find_optimal_threshold(scores[mask], labels[mask])
347	        pred = int(scores[i] > t)
348	        correct += (pred == labels[i])
349

370	# ── Plotting ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

372	def plot_results(setting_result, subgroup_result, settings_df, subgroups_df, out_path):
373	    """Save combined ROC + scatter plots."""
374	    import matplotlib

1	"""Generate paper-quality ROC figure for Appendix (fig4_roc.png)."""
2	import numpy as np
3	import pandas as pd
4	from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score, roc_curve
5	import matplotlib
6	matplotlib.use('Agg')
7	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
8	import matplotlib as mpl
9	
10	# Paper style
11	mpl.rcParams.update({
12	    'font.family': 'serif',
13	    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'Times', 'DejaVu Serif'],
14	    'font.size': 10,
15	    'axes.labelsize': 11,
16	    'axes.titlesize': 12,
17	    'legend.fontsize': 9,
18	    'xtick.labelsize': 9,
19	    'ytick.labelsize': 9,
20	    'figure.dpi': 300,
21	    'savefig.dpi': 300,
22	    'axes.linewidth': 0.8,
23	    'xtick.major.width': 0.6,
24	    'ytick.major.width': 0.6,
25	    'lines.linewidth': 1.5,
26	})
27	
28	DATA_DIR = '/home/ubuntu/ec_hte_llm_annotation/artifacts/diagnostic_revision'
29	OUT_PATH = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0520/projects/ec_hte_llm_annotation/docs/paper/figures/fig4_roc.png'
30	
31	df_set = pd.read_csv(f'{DATA_DIR}/setting_level_data.csv')
32	df_sub = pd.read_csv(f'{DATA_DIR}/subgroup_level_data.csv')
33	
34	def safe_roc(y_true, y_score):
35	    y_true = np.array(y_true)
36	    y_score = np.array(y_score)
37	    mask = ~(np.isnan(y_score) | np.isinf(y_score))
38	    y_true, y_score = y_true[mask], y_score[mask]
39	    if len(np.unique(y_true)) < 2:
40	        return None, None, None, np.nan
41	    fpr, tpr, thr = roc_curve(y_true, y_score)
42	    auc = roc_auc_score(y_true, y_score)
43	    return fpr, tpr, thr, auc
44	
45	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.0))
46	
47	colors = {
48	    'delta_pi_only': '#1f77b4',
49	    'delta_pi_x_tau': '#d62728',
50	    'pi_cv': '#2ca02c',

52	
53	# ── Panel A: Setting-level ROC ──

57	    ('delta_pi_x_tau', r'$|\Delta\pi \times \Delta\tau|$', colors['delta_pi_x_tau'], '--'),
58	    ('pi_cv', r'$\mathrm{CV}(\pi_s)$', colors['pi_cv'], '-.'),
59	]:

61	    if fpr is None:
62	        continue
63	    ax.plot(fpr, tpr, color=color, ls=ls, lw=1.5,
64	            label=f'{label} (AUC\,=\,{auc:.2f})')
65	
66	ax.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k:', lw=0.7, alpha=0.4)
67	ax.set_xlabel('False Positive Rate')
68	ax.set_ylabel('True Positive Rate')
69	ax.set_title('(A) Setting-level ROC')
70	ax.legend(loc='lower right', frameon=True, fancybox=False, edgecolor='0.7',
71	          borderpad=0.4, handlelength=2.0)
72	ax.set_xlim(-0.02, 1.02)
